103	function killSource(npcId: number, quick: boolean): Source {

111	const sightSource = (npcId: number): Source => ({ t: 'sight', credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId) });
112	const chatSource = (npcId: number): Source => ({ t: 'chat', credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId) });

122	/** 条目注册表（BestiaryDatabaseNPCsPopulator.Populate 的数据层等价；惰性构建一次） */
123	export function bestiaryEntries(): BestiaryEntryDef[] {
124	  if (entryCache) return entryCache;
125	  const out: BestiaryEntryDef[] = [];
126	  for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
127	    const id = Number(key);
128	    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id === 0 || HIDE.has(id)) continue;
129	    const isTown = BESTIARY_TOWN_IDS.has(id);
130	    // AddEmptyEntries_CrittersAndEnemies_Automated：isLikeATownNPC 走手工城镇表，
131	    // 其余按 CountsAsACritter 分小动物/敌怪
132	    const kind: BestiaryEntryDef['kind'] = isTown ? 'town' : countsAsACritter(id) ? 'critter' : 'enemy';
133	    let sources: Source[];
134	    let goldGate = false;
135	    const goldNormals = GOLD_CRITTER_NORMALS[id];
136	    if (goldNormals) {
137	      // 金小动物：本体遇 + 同族普通种遇；解锁态恒 0/4
138	      sources = [sightSource(id), ...goldNormals.map(sightSource)];
139	      goldGate = true;
140	    } else if (id in CRITTER_PAIR) {
141	      sources = [sightSource(id), sightSource(CRITTER_PAIR[id])];
142	    } else if (id in MULTI_SOURCES) {
143	      sources = MULTI_SOURCES[id].map((s) =>
144	        s === 'kill' ? killSource(id, isQuick(id))
145	          : s === 'chat' ? chatSource(id)
146	            : s[1] === 'kill' ? killSource(s[0], isQuick(s[0])) : chatSource(s[0]));
147	    } else if (id in KILL_CREDIT_FROM) {
148	      // 击杀记账归并族（Populator :600-616）：读母体杀数，quickUnlock=true
149	      sources = [killSource(KILL_CREDIT_FROM[id], true)];
150	    } else if (kind === 'town') {
151	      sources = [chatSource(id)];
152	    } else if (kind === 'critter') {
153	      sources = [sightSource(id)];
154	    } else {
155	      sources = [killSource(id, isQuick(id))];
156	    }
157	    out.push({ creditId: bestiaryCreditId(id), kind, sources, goldGate });
158	  }
159	  entryCache = out;
160	  return out;
161	}
162	
163	function isQuick(id: number): boolean {
164	  return VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id) || COUNTED_AS_BOSS.has(id) || QUICK_UNLOCK_IDS.has(id);
165	}

167	/** CommonEnemyUICollectionInfoProvider.GetUnlockStateByKillCount（:60-85） */
168	export function unlockStateByKillCount(killCount: number, quick: boolean, full: number): UnlockState {
169	  const half = Math.floor(full / 2);
170	  const fifth = Math.floor(full / 5);
171	  if (quick && killCount > 0) return UnlockState.DropsWithRates;
172	  if (killCount >= full) return UnlockState.DropsWithRates;
173	  if (killCount >= half) return UnlockState.DropsNoRates;
174	  if (killCount >= fifth) return UnlockState.Stats;
175	  if (killCount >= 1) return UnlockState.PortraitOnly;
176	  return UnlockState.NotKnown;
177	}
178	
179	export interface BestiarySave {
180	  /** 杀数（creditId → 次数） */
181	  k: Record<string, number>;
182	  /** 遇数（creditId 集） */
183	  s: string[];
184	  /** 聊天解锁（creditId 集） */
185	  c: string[];
186	}
187	
188	/**
189	 * 图鉴统计器（BestiaryUnlocksTracker 1:1）——原版是随世界文件持久化的
190	 * IPersistentPerWorldContent（WorldFile.cs:3399/3405），挂在 World 上。
191	 */
192	export class BestiaryTracker {
193	  readonly kills = new Map<string, number>();
194	  readonly sights = new Set<string>();
195	  readonly chats = new Set<string>();
196	
197	  /** NPCKillsTracker.RegisterKill（杀数上限 999999999） */
198	  registerKill(netId: number): void {
199	    const id = bestiaryCreditId(netId);
200	    const next = Math.min((this.kills.get(id) ?? 0) + 1, KILL_CAP);
201	    this.kills.set(id, next);
202	  }
203	
204	  /** NPCWasNearPlayerTracker.RegisterWasNearby（首遇置位即可，幂等） */
205	  registerSight(netId: number): void {
206	    this.sights.add(bestiaryCreditId(netId));
207	  }
208	
209	  /** NPCWasChatWithTracker.RegisterChatStartWith（幂等） */
210	  registerChat(netId: number): void {
211	    this.chats.add(bestiaryCreditId(netId));
212	  }
213	
214	  private anyGoldSeen(): boolean {
215	    for (const g of GOLD_CRITTER_IDS) {
216	      if (this.sights.has(bestiaryCreditId(g))) return true;
217	    }
218	    return false;
219	  }
220	
221	  /** 条目解锁档（各 UIInfoProvider.GetEntryUICollectionInfo 的数据层等价） */
222	  unlockState(entry: BestiaryEntryDef): UnlockState {
223	    let state = UnlockState.NotKnown;
224	    for (const s of entry.sources) {
225	      const st = s.t === 'kill'
226	        ? unlockStateByKillCount(this.kills.get(s.credit) ?? 0, s.quick, s.full)
227	        : (s.t === 'sight' ? this.sights.has(s.credit) : this.chats.has(s.credit))
228	          ? UnlockState.DropsWithRates
229	          : UnlockState.NotKnown;
230	      if (st > state) state = st;
231	    }
232	    if (entry.goldGate && state > UnlockState.NotKnown && !this.anyGoldSeen()) {
233	      // GoldCritterUICollectionInfoProvider.TryFindingOneGoldCritterThatIsAlreadyUnlocked
234	      return UnlockState.NotKnown;
235	    }
236	    return state;
237	  }
238	
239	  /** 条目是否已解锁（完成度分母口径：UnlockState > NotKnown） */
240	  isUnlocked(entry: BestiaryEntryDef): boolean {
241	    return this.unlockState(entry) > UnlockState.NotKnown;
242	  }
243	
244	  /** Main.GetBestiaryProgressReport（:65003-65020）——percent=已解锁/总数，总数 0 → 1 */
245	  completion(): { total: number; unlocked: number; percent: number } {
246	    const entries = bestiaryEntries();
247	    let unlocked = 0;
248	    for (const e of entries) if (this.isUnlocked(e)) unlocked++;
249	    return {
250	      total: entries.length,
251	      unlocked,
252	      percent: entries.length === 0 ? 1 : unlocked / entries.length,
253	    };
254	  }
255	
256	  toSave(): BestiarySave {
257	    const k: Record<string, number> = {};
258	    for (const [id, n] of this.kills) k[id] = n;
259	    return { k, s: [...this.sights], c: [...this.chats] };
260	  }
261	
262	  static fromSave(data: BestiarySave | undefined): BestiaryTracker {
263	    const t = new BestiaryTracker();
264	    if (!data) return t;
265	    if (data.k) for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(data.k)) {
266	      if (typeof n === 'number' && n > 0) t.kills.set(id, Math.min(n, KILL_CAP));
267	    }
268	    if (data.s) for (const id of data.s) t.sights.add(id);
269	    if (data.c) for (const id of data.c) t.chats.add(id);
270	    return t;
271	  }
272	
273	  /** WorldGen.cs:7068 Reset（世界重生成时清零） */
274	  reset(): void {
275	    this.kills.clear();
276	    this.sights.clear();
277	    this.chats.clear();
278	  }
279	}
280	
281	/** NPC.cs:79701 IsNPCValidForBestiaryKillCredit（121 不计；EoW 三段需 boss 实例旗） */
282	export function isNpcValidForBestiaryKillCredit(vanillaId: number, isBoss: boolean): boolean {
283	  if (vanillaId === 121) return false;
284	  if (vanillaId === 13 || vanillaId === 14 || vanillaId === 15) return isBoss;
285	  return true;
286	}
287	

```

689		private void MoveToVisuallyClosestPoint(List<UILinkPoint> lostrefpoints)
690		{
691			_ = UILinkPointNavigator.Points;
692			Vector2 mouseScreen = Main.MouseScreen;
693			UILinkPoint uILinkPoint = null;
694			foreach (UILinkPoint lostrefpoint in lostrefpoints)
695			{
696				if (uILinkPoint == null || Vector2.Distance(mouseScreen, uILinkPoint.Position) > Vector2.Distance(mouseScreen, lostrefpoint.Position))
697				{
698					uILinkPoint = lostrefpoint;
699				}
700			}
701			if (uILinkPoint != null)
702			{
703				UILinkPointNavigator.ChangePoint(uILinkPoint.ID);
704			}
705		}

707		private void SetupPointsForEntryGrid(ref int currentID, List<SnapPoint> pts, out int gridWidth, out int gridHeight, out UILinkPoint[,] gridPoints)
708		{
709			List<SnapPoint> orderedPointsByCategoryName = GetOrderedPointsByCategoryName(pts, "Entries");
710			_entryGrid.GetEntriesToShow(out gridWidth, out gridHeight, out var _);
711			gridPoints = new UILinkPoint[gridWidth, gridHeight];
712			for (int i = 0; i < orderedPointsByCategoryName.Count; i++)
713			{
714				int num = i % gridWidth;
715				int num2 = i / gridWidth;
716				gridPoints[num, num2] = MakeLinkPointFromSnapPoint(currentID++, orderedPointsByCategoryName[i]);
717			}
718			for (int j = 0; j < gridWidth; j++)
719			{
720				for (int k = 0; k < gridHeight; k++)
721				{
722					UILinkPoint uILinkPoint = gridPoints[j, k];
723					if (j < gridWidth - 1)
724					{
725						UILinkPoint uILinkPoint2 = gridPoints[j + 1, k];
726						if (uILinkPoint != null && uILinkPoint2 != null)
727						{
728							PairLeftRight(uILinkPoint, uILinkPoint2);
729						}
730					}
731					if (k < gridHeight - 1)
732					{
733						UILinkPoint uILinkPoint3 = gridPoints[j, k + 1];
734						if (uILinkPoint != null && uILinkPoint3 != null)
735						{
736							PairUpDown(uILinkPoint, uILinkPoint3);
737						}
738					}
739				}
740			}
741		}

749			{

753			}

784			for (int i = 0; i < orderedPointsByCategoryName.Count; i++)
785			{

789			}

815		private static List<SnapPoint> GetOrderedPointsByCategoryName(List<SnapPoint> pts, string name)
816		{
817			return (from x in pts
818				where x.Name == name
819				orderby x.Id
820				select x).ToList();
821		}
822	
823		private void PairLeftRight(UILinkPoint leftSide, UILinkPoint rightSide)
824		{
825			leftSide.Right = rightSide.ID;
826			rightSide.Left = leftSide.ID;
827		}
828	
829		private void PairUpDown(UILinkPoint upSide, UILinkPoint downSide)
830		{
831			upSide.Down = downSide.ID;
832			downSide.Up = upSide.ID;
833		}
834	
835		private UILinkPoint MakeLinkPointFromSnapPoint(int id, SnapPoint snap)
836		{
837			UILinkPointNavigator.SetPosition(id, snap.Position);
838			UILinkPoint uILinkPoint = UILinkPointNavigator.Points[id];
839			uILinkPoint.Unlink();
840			return uILinkPoint;
841		}

89	    this.buildCats();

91	    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {

113	    const cats = document.createElement('div');

115	    const list = ['sound', 'ui', 'game', ...(this.flow.onQuickLoad ? ['data'] : [])];
116	    for (const c of list) {
117	      const div = document.createElement('div');
118	      div.dataset.cat = c;
119	      div.textContent = this.catLabel(c);
120	      div.addEventListener('click', () => { this.cat = c; this.build(); });
121	      cats.appendChild(div);
122	    }
123	    this.panel.querySelector('.sw-set-cats')!.replaceWith(cats);
124	  }
125	
126	  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;
127	
128	  private close(fn: () => void) {
129	    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
130	    this.unsubLang?.();
131	    this.panel.remove();
132	    fn();
133	  }
134	
135	  private slider(label: string, min: number, max: number, step: number, get: () => number, set: (v: number) => void) {
136	    const row = document.createElement('div');
137	    row.className = 'sw-set-row';
138	    const span = document.createElement('span');
139	    span.textContent = label;
140	    const input = document.createElement('input');
141	    input.type = 'range';
142	    input.min = String(min); input.max = String(max); input.step = String(step);
143	    input.value = String(get());
144	    const val = document.createElement('span');
145	    val.className = 'val';
146	    val.textContent = String(Math.round(get() * 100));
147	    input.addEventListener('input', () => {
148	      const v = Number(input.value);
149	      set(v);
150	      val.textContent = String(Math.round(v * 100));
151	    });
152	    row.append(span, input, val);
153	    return row;
154	  }
155	
156	  private toggle(label: string, get: () => boolean, set: (v: boolean) => void) {
157	    const row = document.createElement('label');
158	    row.className = 'sw-set-row';
159	    const span = document.createElement('span');
160	    span.textContent = label;
161	    const input = document.createElement('input');
162	    input.type = 'checkbox';
163	    input.checked = get();
164	    input.addEventListener('change', () => set(input.checked));
165	    row.append(span, input);
166	    return row;
167	  }
168	
169	  /** 双态枚举行（点击在两态间切换；智能光标 Toggle/Hold 模式用） */
170	  private modeRow(label: string, _get: () => string, value: () => string, cycle: () => void) {
171	    const row = document.createElement('div');
172	    row.className = 'sw-set-row';
173	    const span = document.createElement('span');
174	    span.textContent = label;
175	    const btn = document.createElement('button');
176	    btn.className = 'sw-set-back';
177	    btn.style.padding = '4px 14px';
178	    btn.textContent = value();
179	    btn.addEventListener('click', () => { cycle(); btn.textContent = value(); });
180	    row.append(span, btn);
181	    return row;
182	  }
183	
184	  /** 语言选择网格:显示名取各语言包自带的 Language.* 键(原版语义) */
185	  private langGrid() {
186	    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
187	    const title = document.createElement('div');
188	    title.className = 'sw-set-lang-title';
189	    title.textContent = Lang.text('LegacyMenu.102'); // 选择语言
190	    const grid = document.createElement('div');
191	    grid.className = 'sw-set-lang-grid';
192	    const current = options.data.lang;
193	    for (const c of Lang.cultures) {
194	      const b = document.createElement('button');
195	      const name = Lang.text(c.displayNameKey);
196	      b.textContent = Lang.has(c.displayNameKey) ? name : c.name;
197	      if (c.name === current) b.classList.add('active');
198	      b.addEventListener('click', async () => {
199	        if (c.name === options.data.lang) return;
200	        const prev = options.data.lang;
201	        b.disabled = true;
202	        // 先写内存值:Lang.setLanguage 的 emit 会触发 rebuild 重建本网格,
203	        // 高亮读 options.data.lang——后写会拿到旧值,激活态留在上一语言
204	        options.data.lang = c.name;
205	        const ok = await Lang.setLanguage(c.name);
206	        b.disabled = false;
207	        if (ok) {
208	          await options.set('lang', c.name); // 持久化(值已同步,仅落盘)
209	        } else {
210	          options.data.lang = prev; // 失败回滚
211	          this.rebuild();
212	        }
213	      });
214	      b.dataset.lang = c.name;
215	      grid.appendChild(b);
216	    }
217	    wrap.append(title, grid);
218	    const hint = document.createElement('div');
219	    hint.className = 'sw-set-hint';
220	    hint.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.FontHint');
221	    wrap.append(hint);
222	    return wrap;
223	  }
224	
225	  private build() {
226	    this.panel.querySelectorAll('.sw-set-cats div').forEach((el) => {
227	      (el as HTMLElement).classList.toggle('active', (el as HTMLElement).dataset.cat === this.cat);
228	    });
229	    this.content.innerHTML = '';
230	    const d = options.data;
231	    if (this.cat === 'sound') {
232	      this.content.append(
233	        this.slider(Lang.text('LegacyMenu.99'), 0, 1, 0.05, () => d.musicVol, (v) => { d.musicVol = v; this.applySound(); void options.set('musicVol', v); }),
234	        this.slider(Lang.text('LegacyMenu.98'), 0, 1, 0.05, () => d.sfxVol, (v) => { d.sfxVol = v; this.applySound(); void options.set('sfxVol', v); }),
235	      );
236	    } else if (this.cat === 'ui') {
237	      this.content.append(
238	        this.slider(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.UiScale'), 0.75, 1.5, 0.05, () => d.uiScale, (v) => void options.set('uiScale', v).then(() => this.applyUi())),
239	        this.langGrid(),
240	      );
241	      const hint = document.createElement('div');
242	      hint.className = 'sw-set-hint';
243	      hint.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.UiScaleHint');
244	      this.content.append(hint);
245	    } else if (this.cat === 'game') {
246	      this.content.append(
247	        this.toggle(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.DevMode'), () => d.devMode, (v) => void options.set('devMode', v)),
248	        // 背景 开/关（Main.BackgroundEnabled Main.cs:790，原版视频设置项 Lang.menu[100/101]；
249	        // 天气阴天渐变 cloudBGAlpha 的门，Game.updateWeather 注入 WeatherCtx.backgroundEnabled）
250	        this.toggle(Lang.menu(100).replace(/[:：].*$/, ''), () => d.backgrounds, (v) => void options.set('backgrounds', v)),
251	        // 图块在风中摆动（原版视频设置 SettingsEnabled_TilesSwayInWind；WindSway 系统总门）
252	        this.toggle('图块在风中摆动', () => d.swayInWind, (v) => void options.set('swayInWind', v)),
253	        // 智能光标（原版 IngameOptions :1418 Smart Cursor 段）：模式 Toggle/Hold + 三开关
254	        this.modeRow(
255	          Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartCursorMode'),
256	          () => d.smartCursorMode,
257	          () => Lang.text(d.smartCursorMode === 'toggle'
258	            ? 'Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartCursorToggle'
259	            : 'Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartCursorHold'),
260	          () => void options.set('smartCursorMode', d.smartCursorMode === 'toggle' ? 'hold' : 'toggle'),
261	        ),
262	        this.toggle(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartAxeAfterPickaxe'), () => d.smartAxeAfterPickaxe, (v) => void options.set('smartAxeAfterPickaxe', v)),
263	        this.toggle(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartBlocks'), () => d.smartBlocksEnabled, (v) => void options.set('smartBlocksEnabled', v)),
264	        this.toggle(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartHoldRelease'), () => d.smartHoldCanReleaseMidUse, (v) => void options.set('smartHoldCanReleaseMidUse', v)),
265	      );
266	      const hint = document.createElement('div');
267	      hint.className = 'sw-set-hint';
268	      hint.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.DevModeHint');
269	      this.content.append(hint);
270	      const scHint = document.createElement('div');
271	      scHint.className = 'sw-set-hint';
272	      scHint.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartCursorHint');
273	      this.content.append(scHint);
274	    } else if (this.cat === 'data' && this.flow.onQuickLoad) {
275	      const btns = document.createElement('div');
276	      btns.className = 'sw-set-btns';
277	      const mk = (label: string, fn: () => void) => {
278	        const b = document.createElement('button');
279	        b.textContent = label;
280	        b.addEventListener('click', () => this.close(fn));
281	        btns.appendChild(b);
282	      };
283	      mk(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Data.ContinueSave'), () => this.flow.onQuickLoad!());
284	      mk(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Data.LoadFile'), () => this.flow.onLoadFile?.());
285	      mk(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Data.ImportWld'), () => this.flow.onImportWld?.());
286	      this.content.append(btns);
287	    }
288	  }
289	
290	  /** 语言已切换:重建标题/分栏/内容(保留 this.cat) */
291	  private rebuild() {
292	    (this.panel.querySelector('.sw-set-title') as HTMLElement).textContent = Lang.text('LegacyMenu.14');
293	    (this.panel.querySelector('.sw-set-back') as HTMLElement).textContent = Lang.text('LegacyMenu.6');
294	    this.buildCats();
295	    this.build();
296	  }
297	
298	  /** 音量下发（Audio 实例由 main.ts 注入过 options.onChange，这里同步 UISfx） */
299	  private applySound() {
300	    // UISfx 共享实例的 master
301	    (UISfx as unknown as { sfx: { master: number } }).sfx.master = options.data.sfxVol;
302	  }
303	
304	  private applyUi() {
305	    UIScale.userScale = options.data.uiScale;
306	    UIScale.recompute();
307	  }
308	}

9	public class UIBestiaryEntryGrid : UIElement
10	{
11		private List<BestiaryEntry> _workingSetEntries;
12	
13		private MouseEvent _clickOnEntryEvent;
14

16	
17		private int _lastEntry;
18	
19		public event Action OnGridContentsChanged;
20	
21		public UIBestiaryEntryGrid(List<BestiaryEntry> workingSet, MouseEvent clickOnEntryEvent)
22		{
23			Width = new StyleDimension(0f, 1f);
24			Height = new StyleDimension(0f, 1f);
25			_workingSetEntries = workingSet;
26			_clickOnEntryEvent = clickOnEntryEvent;

31	
32		public void UpdateEntries()
33		{
34			_lastEntry = _workingSetEntries.Count;
35		}
36	
37		public void FillBestiarySpaceWithEntries()
38		{
39			RemoveAllChildren();
40			UpdateEntries();
41			GetEntriesToShow(out var maxEntriesWidth, out var maxEntriesHeight, out var maxEntriesToHave);
42			FixBestiaryRange(0, maxEntriesToHave);
43			int atEntryIndex = _atEntryIndex;
44			int num = Math.Min(_lastEntry, atEntryIndex + maxEntriesToHave);
45			List<BestiaryEntry> list = new List<BestiaryEntry>();
46			for (int i = atEntryIndex; i < num; i++)
47			{
48				list.Add(_workingSetEntries[i]);
49			}
50			int num2 = 0;
51			float num3 = 0.5f / (float)maxEntriesWidth;
52			float num4 = 0.5f / (float)maxEntriesHeight;
53			for (int j = 0; j < maxEntriesHeight; j++)
54			{
55				for (int k = 0; k < maxEntriesWidth; k++)
56				{
57					if (num2 >= list.Count)
58					{
59						break;
60					}
61					UIElement uIElement = new UIBestiaryEntryButton(list[num2], isAPrettyPortrait: false);
62					num2++;
63					uIElement.OnLeftClick += _clickOnEntryEvent;
64					uIElement.VAlign = (uIElement.HAlign = 0.5f);
65					uIElement.Left.Set(0f, (float)k / (float)maxEntriesWidth - 0.5f + num3);
66					uIElement.Top.Set(0f, (float)j / (float)maxEntriesHeight - 0.5f + num4);
67					uIElement.SetSnapPoint("Entries", num2, new Vector2(0.2f, 0.7f));
68					Append(uIElement);
69				}
70			}
71		}
72	
73		public override void Recalculate()
74		{
75			base.Recalculate();
76			FillBestiarySpaceWithEntries();
77		}
78	
79		public void GetEntriesToShow(out int maxEntriesWidth, out int maxEntriesHeight, out int maxEntriesToHave)
80		{
81			Rectangle rectangle = GetDimensions().ToRectangle();
82			maxEntriesWidth = rectangle.Width / 72;
83			maxEntriesHeight = rectangle.Height / 72;
84			int num = 0;
85			maxEntriesToHave = maxEntriesWidth * maxEntriesHeight - num;
86		}
87	
88		public string GetRangeText()
89		{
90			GetEntriesToShow(out var _, out var _, out var maxEntriesToHave);
91			int atEntryIndex = _atEntryIndex;
92			int num = Math.Min(_lastEntry, atEntryIndex + maxEntriesToHave);
93			int num2 = Math.Min(atEntryIndex + 1, num);
94			return $"{num2}-{num} ({_lastEntry})";
95		}
96	
97		public void MakeButtonGoByOffset(UIElement element, int howManyPages)
98		{
99			element.OnLeftClick += delegate
100			{
101				OffsetLibraryByPages(howManyPages);
102			};
103		}
104	
105		public void OffsetLibraryByPages(int howManyPages)
106		{
107			GetEntriesToShow(out var _, out var _, out var maxEntriesToHave);
108			OffsetLibrary(howManyPages * maxEntriesToHave);
109		}
110	
111		public void OffsetLibrary(int offset)
112		{
113			GetEntriesToShow(out var _, out var _, out var maxEntriesToHave);
114			FixBestiaryRange(offset, maxEntriesToHave);
115			FillBestiarySpaceWithEntries();
116		}
117	
118		private void FixBestiaryRange(int offset, int maxEntriesToHave)
119		{
120			_atEntryIndex = Utils.Clamp(_atEntryIndex + offset, 0, Math.Max(0, _lastEntry - maxEntriesToHave));
121			if (this.OnGridContentsChanged != null)
122			{
123				this.OnGridContentsChanged();
124			}
125		}
126	}

   *  + 结构化克隆双开销；fallback 时 worldStore.load 取回全文走 loadFromJson） */
  async function loadWorldFlow(meta: WorldMeta) {
    currentWorld = meta;
    await loadFromKey(`sandboxworld.world.${meta.id}`);
  }

  /** 创建新世界：原版生成页（双进度条+实时地图预览）→ 注册槽位 → 进游戏 */
  async function createWorldFlow(cfg: { name: string; seed: string; w: number; h: number; difficulty: number; evil: -1 | 0 | 1 }) {
    const loadState = new UIWorldLoadState(cfg.evil);
    VUI.setState(loadState);
    const g = makeGame();
    await g.newWorld(cfg.seed || String(Date.now()), cfg.w, cfg.h,
      (label, p) => loadState.setProgress(label, p),
      {
        name: cfg.name,
        evil: cfg.evil,
        onWorldPartial: (world) => loadState.attachWorld(world), // 主线程 fallback：列扫描预览
        onPreview: (f) => loadState.attachPreview(f),            // worker 路径：位图预览
      });
    // onWorldReady 已进游戏；登记世界槽位并保存初始数据
    const meta = await worldStore.register({
      name: cfg.name, seed: cfg.seed, w: cfg.w, h: cfg.h,
      difficulty: cfg.difficulty, evil: cfg.evil,
    });
    currentWorld = meta;
    doSave();
  }

  /** 设置面板（主菜单含数据栏；游戏内不显示数据栏） */
  function openSettings(inGame: boolean) {
    new SettingsPanel(root, {
      ...(inGame ? {} : {
        onQuickLoad: () => void quickLoad(),
        onLoadFile: () => {
          fileInput.onchange = () => {
            const f = fileInput.files?.[0];
            if (f) void f.text().then(loadFromJson);
            fileInput.value = '';
          };
          fileInput.click();
        },
        onImportWld: () => {
          wldInput.onchange = () => {
            const f = wldInput.files?.[0];
            if (f) void f.arrayBuffer().then((ab) => importWld(new Uint8Array(ab)));
            wldInput.value = '';
          };
          wldInput.click();
        },
      }),
      onBack: () => { /* 面板自毁 */ },
    });
  }

  function showTitle() {
    audio.play('title');
    ui.closeAll();
    if (!menuBg) {
      menuBg = new MenuBackground(root);
      (window as unknown as { __swMenuBg?: MenuBackground }).__swMenuBg = menuBg; // 调试句柄

    titleMenu?.destroy();

   *  querySelector('button') 仍命中垫片——旧探针脚本零修改。 */
  function installLegacyShim() {
    if (legacyShim) return;
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:2px;height:2px;opacity:0.01;z-index:1;';
    const sel = document.createElement('select');
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="4200x1200">小</option><option value="6400x1800" selected>中</option><option value="8400x2400">大</option>';
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.textContent = '创建新世界';
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const [w, h] = (sel.value || '6400x1800').split('x').map(Number);
      void newWorld('', w, h);
    });
    div.append(sel, btn);
    root.appendChild(div);

window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  const game = flow.game;

    panel.style.cssText = 'pointer-events:auto; position:fixed; left:50%; top:50%; transform:translate(-50%,-50%); width:280px;';

    bRes.onclick = () => { panel.remove(); handlers.onResume(); };
    const bSave = el('button', 'sw-btn', Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.UI.SaveGame'));
    bSave.onclick = () => handlers.onSave();
    const bSet = el('button', 'sw-btn', Lang.text('LegacyMenu.14'));
    bSet.onclick = () => handlers.onSettings?.();
    const bQuit = el('button', 'sw-btn', Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.UI.BackToMenu'));
    bQuit.onclick = () => { panel.remove(); handlers.onQuit(); };
    panel.append(bRes, bSave, bSet, bQuit);
    this.root.appendChild(panel);
    return panel;
  }

// Terarria1456/NPC.cs SetDefaults :17338-17367（aiStyle 9，字段对齐 30/112 同族；贴图
// NPC_665/666.png 已在 sprites，此前纯数据缺口）。
// 星璇塔增量（2026-08-11）：403/404（星尘蠕虫身/尾，字段复制 402 改宽高——1456 无独立
// SetDefaults，原版由 DrawNPC case 402 自绘 trail；本仓蠕虫链 spawnWormChain 需要
// 头+1/头+2 条目）、408（小水流入侵者，复制 407 缩小——1456 无独立 SetDefaults）。
// aiStyle 与 AI_XXX 方法的对应见 NPC.cs AI() 分发；此处先覆盖高频家族，
// 未移植 AI 家族的 aiStyle 回退到最接近的已移植行为（注释标注）。
import vanillaNpcsJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla-npcs.json';
import vanillaDataJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';
import { TILE_DEFS } from './tiles';

/** tile id → key 反查（biomeAt 群系判定用） */
const TILE_KEY_NAME: string[] = TILE_DEFS.map((d) => d.key);

// （旧掉落表已退役：掉落走 src/drops/NpcDrops.ts 规则树——
//  tools/extract-npcdrops.mjs 结构化提取 ItemDropDatabase.cs，含专家/大师/条件/链语义）

/** 原版物品 id → 本仓库 item key（vanilla.json 的 key 是 PascalCase，ITEM_BY_KEY 多为 snake_case；
 *  未注册的返回 null 跳过） */
export const vanillaItemKey = (() => {
  const map = new Map<number, string | null>();
  const items = (vanillaDataJson as unknown as { items: Record<string, { key?: string }> }).items ?? {};
  return (itemId: number): string | null => {
    if (map.has(itemId)) return map.get(itemId)!;
    const meta = items[String(itemId)];
    let key: string | null = null;
    if (meta?.key) {
      const snake = meta.key.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').toLowerCase();
      if (ITEM_BY_KEY[snake] != null) key = snake;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[meta.key] != null) key = meta.key;
      // vi_NNN 导入物品（items.ts 的全量原版物品命名）：vi_<id> 或 vi_<id>_<snake>
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}`;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}_${snake}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}_${snake}`;
    }
    map.set(itemId, key);
    return key;
  };
})();

export interface VanillaNpc {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  frames: number;
  lifeMax: number;
  damage: number;
  defense: number;
  knockBackResist: number;
  aiStyle: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  npcSlots: number;
  noGravity: boolean;
  noTileCollide: boolean;
  friendly: boolean;
  townNPC: boolean;
  HitSound: string;   // SoundID 名（NPCHitN / NPCDeathN）
  DeathSound: string;
  scale: number;
  /** SetDefaults alpha：每类型静态不透明度基线（渲染 1-alpha/255，NPC.Opacity；无通用渐隐） */
  alpha?: number;
  /** SetDefaults color：非 default 时 Main.DrawNPC 二次绘制同贴图（GetColor 逐像素乘法贴轮廓） */
  color?: number[];  // [r, g, b, a]
  critter?: boolean;  // NPCID.Sets.CountsAsCritter 小动物（tools/extract-critters.mjs 提取）
}

export const VANILLA_NPCS = vanillaNpcsJson as unknown as Record<string, VanillaNpc>;

/** GetGemSquirrelToSpawn（NPC.cs:5611-5623）/ GetGemBunnyToSpawn（:5591-5603）：
 *  纯加权随机表（Diamond 5%/Amber 8%/Ruby 10%/Emerald 12%/Sapphire 16%/Topaz 21%/Amethyst 28%）
 *  ——与就近宝石 tile、世界档位完全无关 */
export function getGemSquirrelToSpawn(rand: () => number): number {
  const num = Math.floor(rand() * 100);
  if (num < 5) return 644;
  if (num < 13) return 645;
  if (num < 23) return 643;
  if (num < 35) return 642;
  if (num < 51) return 641;
  if (num < 72) return 640;
  return 639;
}
export function getGemBunnyToSpawn(rand: () => number): number {
  const num = Math.floor(rand() * 100);
  if (num < 5) return 651;
  if (num < 13) return 652;
  if (num < 23) return 650;
  if (num < 35) return 649;
  if (num < 51) return 648;
  if (num < 72) return 647;
  return 646;
}

export function vanillaNpc(id: number): VanillaNpc | null {
  return VANILLA_NPCS[String(id)] ?? null;
}

// ================= 城镇 NPC（TownNPC 实体用） =================
// key → 原版 NPCID（Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/NPCID.cs:11099+）；
// extra = NPCID.Sets.ExtraFramesCount（NPCID.cs:4831）——
// 行走帧循环区间的回卷上界：帧 >= frames-extra 时回帧 2（NPC.cs FindFrame L70244）
export const TOWN_NPC_IDS: Record<string, { id: number; extra: number }> = {
  guide: { id: 22, extra: 10 },
  old_man: { id: 37, extra: 2 },   // 守卫老人(地牢门口;夜晚诅咒召唤骷髅王)
  merchant: { id: 17, extra: 9 },
  nurse: { id: 18, extra: 9 },
  arms_dealer: { id: 19, extra: 9 },
  dryad: { id: 20, extra: 7 },
  demolitionist: { id: 38, extra: 9 },
  clothier: { id: 54, extra: 7 },
  goblin_tinkerer: { id: 107, extra: 9 },
  wizard: { id: 108, extra: 7 },
  mechanic: { id: 124, extra: 9 },
  santa_claus: { id: 142, extra: 9 },
  truffle: { id: 160, extra: 7 },
  steampunker: { id: 178, extra: 9 },
  dyer: { id: 207, extra: 9 },
  party_girl: { id: 208, extra: 9 },
  cyborg: { id: 209, extra: 10 },
  painter: { id: 227, extra: 9 },
  witch_doctor: { id: 228, extra: 10 },
  pirate: { id: 229, extra: 10 },
  stylist: { id: 353, extra: 9 },
  tax_collector: { id: 441, extra: 9 },
  golfer: { id: 588, extra: 9 },
  angler: { id: 369, extra: 9 },      // 渔夫（海滩 376 睡渔夫解救后城镇化；ExtraFramesCount 9）
  tavernkeep: { id: 550, extra: 9 },  // 酒保（击败一次旧日军团后入驻；ExtraFramesCount 9）
  zoologist: { id: 633, extra: 9 },   // BestiaryGirl
  princess: { id: 663, extra: 7 },
  travelling_merchant: { id: 368, extra: 10 },  // 旅行商人（上午随机到访，黄昏离场）
  skeleton_merchant: { id: 453, extra: 9 },     // 骷髅商（洞穴随机出现，随 timeLeft 消失）
};

const TOWN_EXTRA_BY_ID = new Map(Object.values(TOWN_NPC_IDS).map((t) => [t.id, t.extra]));

/** NPC id → 头像贴图索引（原版 NPC.cs:7489 TypeToDefaultHeadIndex 全表——
 *  head 索引 ≠ NPC id！旗帜头像 NPC_Head_{headIndex}） */

  /** 原版表内任意像素偏移取帧（style/显式帧：18px 步长的 frameX/frameY 直用） */
  vframeAt(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number): DrawRect | null {
    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
    if (!m) return null;
    // 与 vframe/vrect 同语义懒加载（ensureVImage）：place_v_* 物品图标走本方法,
    // 此前直接 vimages.get——表未载时不发加载请求,宝箱内家具类物品图标永久回退
    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
    if (!img) return null;
    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + m.grid[0] > img.width || fy + m.grid[1] > img.height) return null;
    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
  }

  /** 原版表内任意矩形（多格物体整体取图，如墓碑 2×2 = 34×34px） */
  vrect(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number, w: number, h: number): DrawRect | null {
    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
    if (!m) return null;
    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
    if (!img) return null;
    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + w > img.width || fy + h > img.height) return null;
    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: w, sh: h };

  vnpc(npcId: number, frameIdx: number): DrawRect | null {
    let m: VanillaNpcMeta | undefined = this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)];
    if (!m) {
      const key = String(npcId);
      m = this.lazyNpcMeta.get(key);
      if (!m) {
        const sheet = `vanilla/NPC_${npcId}.png`;
        const img = this.vimages.get(sheet);
        if (!img) {
          const im = new Image();
          im.onload = () => this.vimages.set(sheet, im);
          im.src = `sprites/${sheet}`;
          return null;
        }
        const frames = (vanillaNpcFrames as Record<string, number>)[key] ?? 1;
        const fh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
        const meta: VanillaNpcMeta = { sheet, frameW: img.width, frameH: fh, count: frames };
        this.lazyNpcMeta.set(key, meta);
        m = meta;
      }
    }
    const img = this.vimages.get(m.sheet);
    if (!img) return null;
    const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(m.count - 1, frameIdx));
    return { img, sx: 0, sy: idx * m.frameH, sw: m.frameW, sh: m.frameH };

  /** 按需加载 vanilla 单图(去重;失败静默)。命中返回元素,否则 null。
   *  渲染路径一律用此入口(经 vimg 别名)——直接 vimages.get 会绕过懒加载与
   *  onVImageLoaded 重烘焙钩子(棕榈树干传送消失的根因) */
  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
    if (hit) return hit;
    if (this._iconPending.has(file)) return null;
    this._iconPending.add(file);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => {
      this.vimages.set(file, im);
      this._iconPending.delete(file);
      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
    };
    im.onerror = () => { this._iconPending.delete(file); };
    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
    return null;
  }
  /** 后台预取全部物品图标(保留兼容入口;预载流程请用 awaited 的 preloadIcons) */
  prefetchIcons(): void {
    void this.preloadIcons();
  }

  private _iconsPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;

export interface ResolvedDrop { id: number; n: number; }
export interface NpcDropOutcome {
  drops: ResolvedDrop[];
  /** 规则内 BossBag/local/perPlayer 清零 → 钱币管线跳过 */
  zeroValue: boolean;

type State = 'success' | 'failedRoll' | 'failedCond';

const warned = new Set<string>();
function warnOnce(key: string, msg: string) {
  if (warned.has(key)) return;
  warned.add(key);
  console.warn(`[NpcDrops] ${msg}`);
}

// ============ 条件求值（Conditions.cs 1:1；未知 → false+warn） ============
function downed(ctx: NpcDropCtx, ...ids: number[]): boolean {
  return ids.every((id) => !!ctx.flags[`downed_${id}`]);
}
export function evalCond(name: string, args: unknown[], ctx: NpcDropCtx): boolean {
  const ex = ctx.extras;
  switch (name) {
    case 'NotExpert': return !ctx.isExpert;
    case 'IsExpert': return ctx.isExpert;
    case 'LegacyHack_IsBossAndExpert': return ctx.isExpert;
    case 'LegacyHack_IsBossAndNotExpert': return !ctx.isExpert;
    case 'LegacyHack_IsABoss': return true; // 挂在 boss 自身注册上，击杀即 boss
    case 'Easymode': return !ctx.hardMode;
    case 'IsHardmode': return ctx.hardMode;
    case 'NotRemixSeedHardmode': return ctx.hardMode;
    case 'RemixSeedHardmode': return false;
    case 'RemixSeed': return false;
    case 'NotRemixSeed': return true;
    case 'NeverTrue': return false;
    case 'NotFromStatue': return !ctx.statueSpawned; // Conditions.NotFromStatue
    case 'IsBloodMoonAndNotFromStatue': return !!ex?.bloodMoon && !ctx.statueSpawned;
    case 'IsCrimsonAndNotExpert': return ctx.crimson && !ctx.isExpert;
    case 'IsCorruptionAndNotExpert': return !ctx.crimson && !ctx.isExpert;
    case 'IsChristmas': return !!ex?.xmas;
    case 'HalloweenWeapons':
    case 'HalloweenGoodieBagDrop': return !!ex?.halloween;
    case 'XmasPresentDrop': return !!ex?.xmas;
    case 'DontStarveIsUp': return false;
    case 'TenthAnniversaryIsUp': return false;
    case 'DontStarveIsNotUp': case 'TenthAnniversaryIsNotUp': return true;
    case 'DownedPlantera': return downed(ctx, 262);
    case 'FirstTimeKillingPlantera': return !downed(ctx, 262); // 掉落先于 downed 写入 → 首杀语义
    case 'DownedAllMechBosses': return downed(ctx, 134, 125, 127);
    case 'BeatAnyMechBoss': return downed(ctx, 134) || downed(ctx, 125) || downed(ctx, 127);
    case 'MechdusaKill':
      // Conditions.cs:579：首需 SpecialSeedFeatures.Mechdusa（Mekdusa 秘密种子），
      // 再需三王一眼 AnyNPCs 扫描。秘密种子系统未实装 → 恒 false
      return false;
    case 'FromCertainWaveAndAbove': {
      // Conditions.cs:249-261：NPC.waveNumber >= neededWave（不查事件——无事件时
      // waveNumber=0 恒不满足；注册仅在冰女王专属掉落 1914 上，neededWave=15）
      return (ctx.moonEvent?.wave ?? 0) >= Number(args[0] ?? 0);
    }
    case 'PumpkinMoonDropGatingChance': case 'FrostMoonDropGatingChance': {
      // 稀有掉落总门（Conditions.cs:55-121）：霜月基数 28 / 南瓜月 24；
      // 专家 wave+5；denom = ⌊(base-wave)/2.5⌋，专家再 -2(霜)/-1(南瓜)；下限 1；
      // RollLuck(denom)==0 才放行（luck=0 ≡ rng.Next(denom)==0）
      const me = ctx.moonEvent;
      const frost = name === 'FrostMoonDropGatingChance';
      if (!me || me.kind !== (frost ? 1 : 2)) return false;
      let wave = me.wave;
      if (ctx.isExpert) wave += 5;
      let denom = Math.trunc(((frost ? 28 : 24) - wave) / 2.5);
      if (ctx.isExpert) denom -= frost ? 2 : 1;
      if (denom < 1) denom = 1;
      return rollLuck(ctx, denom, 1);

// ============ 规则求值（ItemDropResolver.cs 语义） ============
/** Player.RollLuck(denom, luck=0) ≡ rng.Next(denom) */
function rollLuck(ctx: NpcDropCtx, denom: number, num: number): boolean {
  if (denom <= 1) return true;
  const luck = ctx.luck ?? 0;
  if (luck !== 0 && ctx.rng.next() < Math.abs(luck)) {
    // Luck.RollLuck（Luck.cs:6）：幸运 → 先掷 [denom/2, denom) 再掷（<num 更易命中）；
    // 负幸运反向 → [denom, denom*2) 更难命中
    const inner = luck > 0
      ? ctx.rng.int(Math.floor(denom / 2), denom - 1)
      : ctx.rng.int(denom, denom * 2 - 1);
    return ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1, inner) - 1) < num;
  }
  return ctx.rng.int(0, denom - 1) < num;
}

function pushDrop(out: NpcDropOutcome, id: number, n: number) {
  if (n > 0) out.drops.push({ id, n });
}

function condPass(rule: Rule, ctx: NpcDropCtx): boolean | null {
  if (!rule.cond) return true;
  const pass = evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx);
  return pass;
}

function evalRule(rule: Rule, ctx: NpcDropCtx, out: NpcDropOutcome): State {
  let state: State;
  switch (rule.k) {
    case 'common': {
      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom, rule.num ?? 1)) {
        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'rerolls': {
      // CommonDropWithRerolls：rerolls+1 次掷骰任一成功
      let hit = false;
      for (let i = 0; i <= (rule.rerolls ?? 0); i++) hit = hit || rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom, rule.num ?? 1);
      if (hit) { pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1)); state = 'success'; }
      else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'cond': {
      // ItemDropWithConditionRule：条件不过 = DoesntFillConditions（failedRoll 链不进）
      if (!condPass(rule, ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom, rule.num ?? 1)) {
        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'oneOf': {
      // OneFromOptionsDropRule：过掷后等权取一
      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom, rule.num ?? 1)) {
        pushDrop(out, rule.ids[ctx.rng.int(0, rule.ids.length - 1)], 1);
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'expert': { // DropBasedOnExpertMode：normal/expert 二选一委托
      state = evalRule(ctx.isExpert ? rule.expert : rule.normal, ctx, out);
      break;
    }
    case 'master': { // DropBasedOnMasterMode
      state = evalRule(ctx.isMaster ? rule.master : rule.def, ctx, out);
      break;
    }
    case 'masterExpert': { // DropBasedOnMasterAndExpertMode：master>expert>def
      state = evalRule(ctx.isMaster ? rule.master : ctx.isExpert ? rule.expert : rule.def, ctx, out);
      break;
    }
    case 'bossBag': {
      // BossBag = DropBasedOnExpertMode(DropNothing, local(bag)+value=0)；
      // DropNothing 状态 = DoesntFillConditions（非专家分支不进 OnSuccess 链）
      if (rule.cond && !evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
      if (ctx.isExpert) {
        // local 规则 rng.Next(1)<1 恒过 → 掉袋 + 清 value
        pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1);
        out.zeroValue = true;
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedCond';
      break;
    }
    case 'masterCommon': { // ByCondition(IsMasterMode, item)（非 master = DoesntFillConditions）
      if (ctx.isMaster) { pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1); state = 'success'; }
      else state = 'failedCond';
      break;
    }
    case 'masterAll': { // DropBasedOnMasterMode(DropNothing, perPlayer(item, denom, IsMasterMode))
      if (ctx.isMaster) {
        // perPlayer 规则 TryDropping 恒 Success，掷骰在掉落内部决定
        if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom ?? 1, 1)) pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1);
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedCond';
      break;
    }
    case 'local': case 'perPlayer': {
      // DropLocalPerClientAndResetsNPCMoneyTo0：rng.Next 掷骰 + value 清零（单人化=直接掉）
      if (rule.cond && !evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
      if (ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1, rule.denom ?? 1) - 1) < 1) {
        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
        out.zeroValue = true;
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'gate': {
      // LeadingConditionRule：CanDrop=cond、DropAttempt 恒 success → 放行 OnSuccess 链
      state = condPass(rule, ctx) ? 'success' : 'failedCond';
      break;
    }
    case 'nothing': state = 'failedCond'; break; // DropNothing 状态 = DoesntFillConditions
    case 'mechSpawn': {
      // MechBossSpawnersDropRule：value>0 && hardMode && 未三王齐灭 → 各 1/2500
      const can = ctx.value > 0 && ctx.hardMode && !downed(ctx, 134, 125, 127);
      if (!can) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
      if (!downed(ctx, 134) && rollLuck(ctx, 2500, 1)) { pushDrop(out, 556, 1); state = 'success'; break; }
      if (!downed(ctx, 125) && rollLuck(ctx, 2500, 1)) { pushDrop(out, 544, 1); state = 'success'; break; }
      if (!downed(ctx, 127) && rollLuck(ctx, 2500, 1)) { pushDrop(out, 557, 1); state = 'success'; break; }
      state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'slimeBody': case 'statueMimic': {
      // SlimeBodyItemDropRule/StatueMimicItemDropRule：依赖 npc.ai[1]（雕像携带物品）
      // 我们无雕像产怪 → 恒不触发
      state = 'failedCond';
      break;
    }
    case 'dropOneByOne': {
      // DropOneByOne（DropOneByOne.cs TryDroppingItem 1:1）。注册仅四塔碎片
      // （ItemDropDatabase.cs RegisterBoss_LunarTowers :610-629），参数 json 未数值化、
      // 按 :611-627 硬编码：普通 12-20 块 × 每块 1-3；专家块数同、每块基数 ×1.5
      // （min=(int)1.5=1 / max=(int)4.5=4）+ 每活跃玩家 +1/块。
      // ChanceNumerator/Denominator=1 → RollLuck(1) 恒过
      {
        const expert = ctx.isExpert;
        const chunks = ctx.rng.int(12, 20);                    // Next(12, 21)
        const players = 1;                                     // Main.CurrentFrameFlags.ActivePlayersCount（单人）
        const minStack = (expert ? 1 : 1) + players * (expert ? 1 : 0);
        const maxStack = (expert ? 4 : 3) + players * (expert ? 1 : 0);
        for (let i = 0; i < chunks; i++) {
          pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(minStack, maxStack));
        }
        state = 'success';
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'oneOfRules': { // OneFromRulesRule：rng.Next(denom) 过后等权取一，
      // 被选中的 option 走完整子求值（含其自身链）；规则状态恒以掷骰为准
      // （OneFromRulesRule.cs TryDroppingItem(info, resolveAction)）
      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom ?? 1, 1)) {
        evalRule(rule.rules[ctx.rng.int(0, rule.rules.length - 1)], ctx, out);
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'listRef': { // List<IItemDropRule>{…}（OneFromRulesRule 参数）
      state = 'success';
      break;
    }
    case 'noRepeat': { // FromOptionsWithoutRepeatsDropRule.cs:27-45：
      // CanDrop 恒 true；dropCount 次不放回抽样（每件 rng.Next(len) 取一后移除），
      // 每件数量恒 1；规则状态恒 Success（无掷骰）
      {
        const pool = [...(rule.ids as number[])];
        for (let i = 0; i < (rule.count ?? 0); i++) {
          if (pool.length <= 0) break;
          const idx = ctx.rng.int(0, pool.length - 1);
          pushDrop(out, pool[idx], 1);
          pool.splice(idx, 1);
        }
        state = 'success';
      }
      break;
    }
    default:
      warnOnce(`rule:${rule.k}`, `未实现的规则 kind ${rule.k}（跳过）`);
      state = 'success';
      break;
  }
  // 链：按父状态门控（Chains.cs TryIfSucceeded/TryIfFailedRandomRoll/TryIfDoesntFillConditions）
  for (const c of rule.chain ?? []) {
    if ((c.on === 'success' && state === 'success')
      || (c.on === 'failedRoll' && state === 'failedRoll')
      || (c.on === 'failedConditions' && state === 'failedCond')) {
      evalRule(c.rule, ctx, out);
    }
  }
  return state;
}

}

/**
 * 规则掉落主入口（ItemDropSolver.TryDropping 语义）：
 * 全局规则 + 该 netID（回退 type）规则按注册序独立求值。
 */
export function resolveDrops(ctx: NpcDropCtx): NpcDropOutcome {
  const out: NpcDropOutcome = { drops: [], zeroValue: false };
  const rules = [
    ...globalRules,
    ...(rulesByNpc.get(ctx.netId) ?? rulesByNpc.get(ctx.type) ?? []),
  ];
  for (const r of rules) evalRule(r, ctx, out);
  return out;
}

// ============ 钱币管线（NPCLoot_DropMoney :80412 1:1，luck=0 → 单掷） ============
export function rollCoins(value: number, ctx: NpcDropCtx, bloodMoon: boolean, midas = false): ResolvedDrop[] {
  // NPCLoot_DropMoney :80414：|luck| 概率 → 掷两次取后者（幸运重掷）
  if ((ctx.luck ?? 0) !== 0 && ctx.rng.next() < Math.abs(ctx.luck!)) {
    return rollCoinsOnce(value, ctx, bloodMoon, midas);
  }
  return rollCoinsOnce(value, ctx, bloodMoon, midas);
}
function rollCoinsOnce(value: number, ctx: NpcDropCtx, bloodMoon: boolean, midas = false): ResolvedDrop[] {
  const rng = ctx.rng;
  let num = value * (1 + rng.int(-20, 75) * 0.01); // Next(-20,76) → [-20,75]
  if (midas) num *= 1 + rng.int(10, 50) * 0.01;   // 金药剂 midas（:80426 Next(10,51) 左闭右开 → int(10,50)；rng.int(a,b)=Next(a,b+1) 约定）
  if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(5, 10) * 0.01;    // 1/2 ×(1.05-1.10)
  if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(10, 20) * 0.01;   // 1/4 ×(1.10-1.20)
  if (rng.int(0, 7) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(15, 30) * 0.01;   // 1/8 ×(1.15-1.30)
  if (rng.int(0, 15) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(20, 40) * 0.01;  // 1/16 ×(1.20-1.40)
  if (rng.int(0, 31) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(25, 50) * 0.01;  // 1/32 ×(1.25-1.50)
  if (rng.int(0, 63) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(50, 100) * 0.01; // 1/64 ×(1.50-2.00)
  if (bloodMoon) num *= 1 + rng.int(0, 100) * 0.01;
  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
  while (Math.floor(num) > 0) {
    if (num > 1000000) {
      let n = Math.floor(num / 1000000);
      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
      let rest = n;
      while (rest > 999) { rest -= 999; drops.push({ id: 74, n: 999 }); }
      num -= 1000000 * n;
      if (rest > 0) drops.push({ id: 74, n: rest });
    } else if (num > 10000) {
      let n = Math.floor(num / 10000);
      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
      num -= 10000 * n;
      if (n > 0) drops.push({ id: 73, n });
    } else if (num > 100) {
      let n = Math.floor(num / 100);
      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
      num -= 100 * n;
      if (n > 0) drops.push({ id: 72, n });
    } else {
      let n = Math.floor(num);
      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 3) + 1));
      n = Math.max(1, n);
      num -= n;
      drops.push({ id: 71, n });
    }
  }
  return drops;
}

// ============ 心/星（NPCLoot_DropCommonLifeAndMana :80332 1:1） ============
export function rollHeartsAndStars(ctx: NpcDropCtx): ResolvedDrop[] {
  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
  if (ctx.type !== 16 && ctx.type !== 81 && ctx.type !== 121
    && ctx.lifeMax > 1 && ctx.damage > 0) {
    if (ctx.rng.int(0, 5) === 0) { // RollLuck(6)==0
      if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.mana < ctx.manaMax) drops.push({ id: 184, n: 1 });
      else if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.hp < ctx.hpMax) drops.push({ id: 58, n: 1 });
    }
    if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.mana < ctx.manaMax) drops.push({ id: 184, n: 1 }); // RollLuck(2)==0
  }
  return drops;
}

// 原版 1.4.5.6 怪物击杀掉落求值运行时（1:1 对照）
// - 规则树数据：src/data/vanilla-npcdrops.json（tools/extract-npcdrops.mjs 从
//   ItemDropDatabase.cs 结构化提取；626 条注册、规则独立按注册序求值）
// - 求值语义：ItemDropResolver.cs:23-58（CanDrop 失败=DoesntFillConditions、
//   掷骰失败=FailedRandomRoll、成功=Success；链按父状态门控）
// - 钱币管线：NPC.cs NPCLoot_DropMoney(:80412) luck=0 单掷 + 8 层随机放大 + 贪心拆币
// - 心/星：NPC.cs NPCLoot_DropCommonLifeAndMana(:80332)
// - Boss 药水+心：DoDeathEvents_DropBossPotionsAndHearts(:79790)
// - 初速度：Item.cs:49325-49337 vx∈[-3,3] vy∈[-4,-1.5]
// 偏差（记录）：luck 已接（Enemy 击杀处注入 Player.luck——src/stats/Luck.ts 聚合，
//   RecalculateLuck :28672-28697；仅未移植来源（瓢虫/微光币等）恒 0）；NotFromStatue 恒 true；
// 天空盒/DST 联动条件恒 false；月事件四条件已实现（ctx.moonEvent 注入，见 evalCond）。
// RedHatSkeletron/MissingTwin/EmpressOfLightIsGenuinelyEnraged 经 ctx.ai3/
// anyNpcOfType 接通（Enemy 注入）；MechdusaKill/NamedNPC/
// EyeOfCthulhuDefeatedAndNoAltarsInWorld 缺子系统恒 false（注释见 evalCond）。
import dropData from '../data/vanilla-npcdrops.json';
import npcValueJson from '../data/vanilla-npcvalue.json';
import type { RNG } from '../core/rng';

/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any */
type Rule = any;
const ENTRIES: Array<{ npcs?: number[]; global?: boolean; rule: Rule }> = (dropData as any).entries;
const SLIME_BODY: Record<string, [number, number]> = (dropData as any).slimeBody;
const NPC_VALUE: Record<string, number> = npcValueJson as any;

/** netID → 规则列表（注册序）；原版 _entriesByNpcNetId */
const rulesByNpc = new Map<number, Rule[]>();
const globalRules: Rule[] = [];
for (const e of ENTRIES) {
  if (e.global) { globalRules.push(e.rule); continue; }
  for (const n of e.npcs ?? []) {
    let l = rulesByNpc.get(n);
    if (!l) rulesByNpc.set(n, l = []);
    l.push(e.rule);
  }
}

/** NPC.value（铜币）。传 netId：负 id 变体（史莱姆 -1..-10 等）有自己的 value 表，
 *  正 netID/无变体传 type 即可 */
export function npcValueOf(netIdOrType: number): number {
  return NPC_VALUE[String(netIdOrType)] ?? 0;
}

	public void Populate(BestiaryDatabase database)
	{
		_currentDatabase = database;
		AddEmptyEntries_CrittersAndEnemies_Automated();
		AddTownNPCs_Manual();
		AddNPCBiomeRelationships_Automated();
		AddNPCBiomeRelationships_Manual();
		AddNPCBiomeRelationships_AddDecorations_Automated();
		ModifyEntriesThatNeedIt();
		RegisterFilters();
		RegisterSortSteps();
	}

	private void RegisterTestEntries()
	{
		Register(BestiaryEntry.Biome("Bestiary_Biomes.Hallow", "Images/UI/Bestiary/Biome_Hallow", Conditions.ReachHardMode));
	}

	private void RegisterSortSteps()
	{
		foreach (IBestiarySortStep item in new List<IBestiarySortStep>
		{
			new SortingSteps.ByUnlockState(),
			new SortingSteps.ByBestiarySortingId(),
			new SortingSteps.Alphabetical(),
			new SortingSteps.ByNetId(),
			new SortingSteps.ByAttack(),
			new SortingSteps.ByDefense(),
			new SortingSteps.ByCoins(),
			new SortingSteps.ByHP(),
			new SortingSteps.ByBestiaryRarity()
		})
		{
			_currentDatabase.Register(item);
		}
	}

	private void RegisterFilters()
	{
		Register(new Filters.ByUnlockState());
		Register(new Filters.ByBoss());
		Register(new Filters.ByRareCreature());
		List<IBestiaryInfoElement> commonInfoElementsForFilters = CommonTags.GetCommonInfoElementsForFilters();
		for (int i = 0; i < commonInfoElementsForFilters.Count; i++)
		{
			Register(new Filters.ByInfoElement(commonInfoElementsForFilters[i]));
		}
	}

	private void ModifyEntriesThatNeedIt_NameOverride(int npcID, string newNameKey)
	{
		BestiaryEntry bestiaryEntry = FindEntryByNPCID(npcID);
		bestiaryEntry.Info.RemoveAll((IBestiaryInfoElement x) => x is NamePlateInfoElement);
		bestiaryEntry.Info.Add(new NamePlateInfoElement(newNameKey, npcID));
		bestiaryEntry.Icon = new UnlockableNPCEntryIcon(npcID, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, newNameKey);
	}

121	    });

function iconUrl(game: Game, id: number): string {
  let url = iconCache.get(id);
  if (!url) {
    // 优先原版素材图标（合成 32×32 dataURL）
    const def = ITEM_DEFS[id];
    if (game.atlas && def) {
      const ar = atlasIconForKey(game.atlas, def.key);
      if (ar) {
        const c = document.createElement('canvas');
        c.width = 32; c.height = 32;
        const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
        cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
        const s = Math.min(32 / ar.sw, 32 / ar.sh);
        const w = ar.sw * s, h = ar.sh * s;
        cx.drawImage(ar.img, ar.sx, ar.sy, ar.sw, ar.sh, (32 - w) / 2, (32 - h) / 2, w, h);
        url = c.toDataURL();
        iconCache.set(id, url);
        return url;

      return c ? c.toDataURL() : '';
    }
    const c = game.assets.itemIcons.get(id);
    url = c ? c.toDataURL() : '';
    if (url) iconCache.set(id, url);  // 无 atlas(永久)才缓存兜底;空串不缓存
  }
  return url;
}

function el<K extends keyof HTMLElementTagNameMap>(tag: K, cls?: string, text?: string): HTMLElementTagNameMap[K] {
  const e = document.createElement(tag);
  if (cls) e.className = cls;
  if (text !== undefined) e.textContent = text;

// 例:Lang.text('UI.Play')、Lang.text('ItemName.HealingPotion')、Lang.text('LegacyWorldGen.9')
import { languageManager } from './LanguageManager';
import { ITEM_KEY_TO_ID, ITEM_NAME_BY_ID, NPC_NAME_BY_ID, BUFF_NAME_BY_ID, PROJECTILE_NAME_BY_ID, TILE_NAME_BY_ID, TILE_NAME_ITEM_BY_SHEET, TILE_NAME_ZH_BY_ID, TILE_NAME_EN_BY_ID, ITEM_NAME_ZH_BY_ID, ITEM_NAME_EN_BY_ID, WALL_NAME_ITEM_BY_WALL, WALL_NAME_ZH_BY_ID, WALL_NAME_EN_BY_ID } from './idNames.generated';

import { TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import { worldgenProgressKey } from './worldgenKeys';

export const Lang = {
  get loaded(): boolean { return languageManager.loaded; },
  /** 语言列表(设置面板数据源,来自 l10n/index.json) */
  get cultures() { return languageManager.cultures; },
  get activeCultureName(): string | null { return languageManager.activeCulture?.name ?? null; },
  get onChange() { return (fn: () => void) => languageManager.onChange(fn); },

  /** 启动初始化(默认 zh-Hans);load() 为旧签名兼容 */
  init(lang?: string | number): Promise<boolean> { return languageManager.init(lang); },
  async load(): Promise<boolean> { return Lang.init(); },

  /** 切换语言并广播(onLanguageChanged);失败返回 false 不改变状态 */
  setLanguage(culture: string | number): Promise<boolean> { return languageManager.setLanguage(culture); },

  /** 点路径取文案(全键 = category.key)。支持 {0}/{1} 占位符。缺失回退 key 本身并 warn 一次 */
  text(path: string, ...args: (string | number)[]): string {
    return languageManager.getTextValue(path, ...args);
  },

  has(path: string): boolean { return languageManager.exists(path); },

  /** 聊天池随机一条（XxxChatter 类目；原版 Language.SelectRandom(Lang.CreateDialogFilter)） */
  chatter(category: string): string | null { return languageManager.randomFromCategory(category); },

  /** CreateDialogFilter 前缀随机（NPC.HasSpecialEventText 的 "XxxSpecialText.Yyy" 族；
   *  原版 Language.SelectRandom(Lang.CreateDialogFilter(prefix))，Lang.cs:425-428） */
  specialText(prefix: string): string | null { return languageManager.randomFromPrefix(prefix); },

  // ---- Legacy 数组等价物(Lang.cs:475-551 InitializeLegacyLocalization)----
  /** gen[i] → LegacyWorldGen.i */
  gen(i: number): string { return languageManager.getTextValue(`LegacyWorldGen.${i}`); },
  /** menu[j] → LegacyMenu.j */
  menu(j: number): string { return languageManager.getTextValue(`LegacyMenu.${j}`); },
  /** inter[k] → LegacyInterface.k */
  inter(k: number): string { return languageManager.getTextValue(`LegacyInterface.${k}`); },
  /** misc[l] → LegacyMisc.l */
  misc(l: number): string { return languageManager.getTextValue(`LegacyMisc.${l}`); },

  /** 世界生成进度文案:pass 名 → LegacyWorldGen 条目(worldgenKeys 映射) */
  worldgenText(passKey: string): string {
    const legacyKey = worldgenProgressKey[passKey];
    return legacyKey != null ? Lang.text(`LegacyWorldGen.${legacyKey}`) : passKey;
  },

  /** Language.RandomFromCategory 便捷门面：类别内等概率随机一条。缺失返回 fallback */
  randomFrom(path: string, fallback = ''): string {
    return languageManager.randomFromCategory(path) ?? fallback;
  },

// vite/vitest 共用配置。
// resolve.extensions 把 .ts 提到 .js 之前：`npm run build` 的 tsc 会在 src/ 里
// 产出与源码同名的 .js 编译产物，默认扩展序（.js 优先）会让 vitest/vite 解析到
// 过期的 .js 而不是 .ts 源码（表现为改了 .ts 测试却跑旧逻辑）。
import { defineConfig, type Plugin } from 'vite';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { existsSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

/** l10n 键完整性自动检查 + 语言包过期自动重建。
 *  ① 自有文案源(tools/l10n-custom)或构建脚本比 public/l10n 产物新 → 先重跑 build-l10n.mjs
 *    （防"补了键没重建包"的静默裸键）。
 *  ② src 全部 Lang.text/has 字面键对 zh-Hans 合并包解析（tools/l10n-audit.mjs）：
 *    vite build 缺键即 fail（构建中断，列出键名与使用位置）；dev 启动仅 console 警告
 *    （不阻塞热更新）。vitest 不走这些钩子，回归由 tests/l10n-audit.test.ts 锁定。 */
function l10nAuditAuto(): Plugin {
  const ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)));
  const sources = [
    join(ROOT, '..', 'tools', 'l10n-custom'),
    join(ROOT, 'scripts', 'build-l10n.mjs'),
    join(ROOT, 'scripts', 'l10n-merge.mjs'),
  ];
  const stale = () => {
    if (!existsSync(join(ROOT, 'public', 'l10n', 'zh-Hans.json'))) return true;
    const srcNew = Math.max(...sources.map((f) => statSync(f).mtimeMs));
    return statSync(join(ROOT, 'public', 'l10n', 'zh-Hans.json')).mtimeMs < srcNew;
  };
  const audit = async () => {
    if (stale()) {
      console.log('[l10n-audit] 语言包源有更新,自动重建…');
      execFileSync('node', [join(ROOT, 'scripts', 'build-l10n.mjs')], { stdio: 'inherit' });
    }
    const { auditL10n } = await import('./tools/l10n-audit.mjs');

	{

		public int Compare(BestiaryEntry x, BestiaryEntry y)
		{
			NPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement nPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement = x.Info.FirstOrDefault((IBestiaryInfoElement element) => element is NPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement) as NPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement;
			NPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement nPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement2 = y.Info.FirstOrDefault((IBestiaryInfoElement element) => element is NPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement) as NPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement;
			if (nPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement == null && nPCNetIdBestiaryInfoElement2 != null)
			{
				return 1;

			{
				return -1;

			{
				return 0;

			{

			return Compare(nPCStatsReportInfoElement, nPCStatsReportInfoElement2);
		}

		public abstract int Compare(NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardX, NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardY);

		public abstract string GetDisplayNameKey();

		private bool IsAStatsCardINeed(IBestiaryInfoElement element)
		{
			if (!(element is NPCStatsReportInfoElement))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}

	public class ByAttack : ByStat
	{
		public override int Compare(NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardX, NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardY)
		{
			return cardY.Damage.CompareTo(cardX.Damage);
		}

		public override string GetDisplayNameKey()
		{
			return "BestiaryInfo.Sort_Attack";
		}
	}

	public class ByDefense : ByStat
	{
		public override int Compare(NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardX, NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardY)
		{
			return cardY.Defense.CompareTo(cardX.Defense);
		}

		public override string GetDisplayNameKey()
		{
			return "BestiaryInfo.Sort_Defense";
		}
	}

	public class ByCoins : ByStat
	{
		public override int Compare(NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardX, NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardY)
		{
			return cardY.MonetaryValue.CompareTo(cardX.MonetaryValue);
		}

		public override string GetDisplayNameKey()
		{
			return "BestiaryInfo.Sort_Coins";
		}
	}

	public class ByHP : ByStat
	{
		public override int Compare(NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardX, NPCStatsReportInfoElement cardY)
		{
			return cardY.LifeMax.CompareTo(cardX.LifeMax);
		}

		public override string GetDisplayNameKey()
		{
			return "BestiaryInfo.Sort_HitPoints";
		}
	}
}

/** 组假 id → 组号 */
function reqIdShift(reqId: number): number { return reqId - 1000000; }

/** 词缀显示名（Lang.prefix → l10n "Prefix.{ConstName}"，缺失回落常量名） */
function prefixDisplayName(prefix: number): string {
  const key = PREFIX_NAMES[String(prefix)];
  if (!key) return '';
  const t = Lang.text(`Prefix.${key}`);
  return t && t !== `Prefix.${key}` ? t : key;
}

/** 词缀后伤害值（Item.Prefix :551：damage = round(damage × dmg)） */
function prefixedDamage(def: (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number], prefix?: number): number {
  if (!def.tool?.damage || !prefix) return def.tool?.damage ?? 0;
  return Math.max(1, Math.round(def.tool.damage * prefixStat(prefix).dmg));
}

        cx.drawImage(ar.img, ar.sx, ar.sy, ar.sw, ar.sh, (32 - w) / 2, (32 - h) / 2, w, h);

```ts
function walk(rule: any, out: DropLine[], gate: number, tag: string | null, cond: string | null) {
  if (!rule || typeof rule !== 'object') return;
  switch (rule.k) {
    case 'common': case 'rerolls': case 'cond': case 'local': case 'perPlayer': {
      const denom = rule.denom ?? 1; const num = rule.num ?? 1;
      add(rule.item, gate * (num / denom), rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1, rule.cond?.name ?? cond, tag);
      break;
    }
    case 'oneOf': for (const id of rule.ids) add(id, gate * ((rule.num ?? 1) / (rule.denom ?? 1)), 1, 1, cond, tag); break;
    case 'expert': walk(rule.normal, out, gate, tag, cond); walk(rule.expert, out, gate, 'IsExpert', cond); break;
    case 'master': walk(rule.def, ...); walk(rule.master, ..., 'IsMasterMode'); break;
    case 'masterExpert': def; expert(→tag IsExpert); master(IsMasterMode); break;
    case 'masterCommon': add(rule.item, gate, 1,1,cond,'IsMasterMode'); break;
    case 'masterAll': add(rule.item, gate*((rule.denom ?? 1)<=1?1:1/(rule.denom??1)), 1,1, cond, 'IsMasterMode'); break;
    case 'bossBag': add(rule.item, gate, 1, 1, rule.cond?.name ?? cond, 'IsExpert'); break;
    case 'gate': walkChains(rule, ...); // gate rule has chain children: pass cond
       Actually 'gate' = LeadingConditionRule with cond; its chain rules are the real drops. Handle: for (const c of rule.chain ?? []) walk(c.rule, out, gate, tag, rule.cond?.name ?? cond);
       return;
    case 'oneOfRules': for (const r of rule.rules ?? []) walk(r, out, gate * (1/(rule.denom ?? 1)), tag, cond); break;
    case 'noRepeat': for (const id of rule.ids ?? []) add(id, gate, 1, 1, cond, tag); break;
    case 'dropOneByOne': add(rule.item, gate, 12, 20, cond, tag); break;
    case 'mechSpawn': [556,544,557].forEach(...) 1/2500; break;
    case 'nothing': case 'listRef': case 'slimeBody': case 'statueMimic': break;
    default: break; // 未知 kind 静默（登记）
  }
  for (const c of rule.chain ?? []) walk(c.rule, out, gate, tag, cond);
}

const BESTIARY_SORT_KEYS: BestiarySortKey[] = ['unlocks', 'id', 'alpha', 'attack', 'defense', 'coins', 'hp'];\n\nexport function sortLabel(key: BestiarySortKey): string {\n  switch (key) {\n    case
